function formatUciMove(fen: string, uci: string): string {
  const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
  if (!match) return uci;
  try {
    const position = new Chess(fen);
    const move = position.move({
      from: match[1] as Square,
      to: match[2] as Square,
      ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
    });
    return move.san;
  } catch {
    return uci;
  }
}

function formatUciPv(fen: string, pv: string[]): string {
  const position = new Chess(fen);
  const san: string[] = [];

    });
    return move.san;
  } catch {
    return uci;
  }
}

function formatUciPv(fen: string, pv: string[]): string {
  const position = new Chess(fen);
  const san: string[] = [];
  for (const uci of pv) {
    const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
    if (!match) break;
    try {
      const move = position.move({
        from: match[1] as Square,
        to: match[2] as Square,
        ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
      });
      san.push(move.san);
    } catch {
      break;
    }
  }
  return san.join(" ");
}

    patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 999_999, session.page + delta)) });
  }

  function goToPage(value: number) {
    if (!session || !Number.isFinite(value)) return;
    patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 1, Math.floor(value))) });
  }

  function moveToPosition(fen: string): boolean {
    const activeSession = session ?? newSession("analysis");
    try {
      const next = new Chess(fen);
      setSession({
        ...activeSession,
        originalFen: fen,
        lineBaseFen: fen,
        currentFen: next.fen(),
        moves: [],
        moveIndex: 0,
      });
      setLastMove(null);
      setSelectedSquare(null);
      setUserArrows([]);
      setNotice("Position loaded. Reset Board returns to this diagram position.");
      return true;
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(`Invalid chess position: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
------------------------------
      setNotice("Position loaded. Reset Board returns to this diagram position.");
      return true;
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(`Invalid chess position: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  function analyzeManualPosition(fen: string): boolean {
    const loaded = moveToPosition(fen);
    if (!loaded) return false;
    setEngineScore(null);
    setEngineMessage("");
    if (!engineEnabled) {
      toggleEngine();
    } else {
      engineRef.current?.analyze(fen, engineDepth);
    }
    setNotice("Manual position loaded. Stockfish analysis started.");
    return true;
  }

  function selectCandidate(candidate: Candidate) {
    if (!session) return;
    setCropEditingId(null);
    cropBaselineRef.current = null;
    patchSession({ selectedDiagramId: candidate.id });

  function patchBoardSession(change: Partial<StudyBoardState>) {
    if (workspaceMode === "opening-explorer") {
      return;
    }

    if (workspaceMode === "analysis") {
      setAnalysisBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
    } else if (workspaceMode === "opening-prep") {
      setOpeningPrepBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
    } else if (studyMode) {
      setStudyBoard((current) => current ? { ...current, ...change } : current);
    } else {
      patchSession(change);
    }
  }
  async function toggleStudyMode() {
    if (!studyMode) {
      if (!session) {
        setError("Open a book before entering study mode.");
        return;
      }
      setStudyBoard({
        lineBaseFen: session.lineBaseFen,
        currentFen: session.currentFen,
        moves: [...session.moves],

function formatUciMove(fen: string, uci: string): string {
  const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
  if (!match) return uci;
  try {
    const position = new Chess(fen);
    const move = position.move({
      from: match[1] as Square,
      to: match[2] as Square,
      ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
    });
    return move.san;
  } catch {
    return uci;
  }

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

def load_backend_env() -> None:
    env_file = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".env"
    if not env_file.is_file():
        return
    for raw_line in env_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines():
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        key, value = line.split("=", 1)
        key, value = key.strip(), value.strip()
        if key and key not in os.environ:
            os.environ[key] = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")

load_backend_env()

@app.post("/detect", response_model=DetectionResponse)
async def detect(image: Annotated[UploadFile, File(...)]) -> DetectionResponse:
    if image.content_type not in {"image/png", "image/jpeg"}:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=415,
            detail="Upload a PNG or JPEG rendered page.",
        )

    content = await image.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
    if len(content) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=413,
            detail="Rendered page image exceeds 12 MiB.",
        )
    if not content:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Image is empty.")

@app.post("/recognize")
async def recognize(image: Annotated[UploadFile, File(...)]) -> dict:
    if image.content_type not in {"image/png", "image/jpeg"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=415, detail="Upload a PNG or JPEG board crop.")
    content = await image.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
    if len(content) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="Board crop exceeds 12 MiB.")
    if not content:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Board crop is empty.")
    decoded = cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(content, dtype=np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if decoded is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Board crop could not be decoded.")
    try:
        return recognize_image(decoded)
    except RecognitionUnavailable as cause:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(cause)) from cause
    except ValueError as cause:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(cause)) from cause

export type OpeningExplorerStats = {
  white: number;
  draws: number;
  black: number;
  total: number;
  moves: OpeningExplorerMove[];
  opening?: { eco?: string; name: string } | null;
};

function count(value: unknown): number {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0
    ? value
    : 0;
}

function opening(value: unknown): OpeningExplorerStats["opening"] {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
  const item = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof item.name !== "string") return null;
------------------------------
  const item = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof item.name !== "string") return null;
  return {
    name: item.name,
    ...(typeof item.eco === "string" ? { eco: item.eco } : {}),
  };
}

export async function fetchOpeningExplorerStats(
  uciMoves: string[],
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<OpeningExplorerStats> {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();
  query.set("variant", "standard");
  query.set("moves", "8");
  if (uciMoves.length) query.set("play", uciMoves.join(","));

  const response = await fetch(
    `/api/lichess/opening-explorer?${query.toString()}`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      credentials: "same-origin",
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      signal,
    },
  );

    black,
    total: white + draws + black,
    moves,
    opening: opening(data.opening),
  };
}

export async function getLichessConnectionStatus(
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<LichessConnectionStatus> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/lichess/status", {
    method: "GET",
    credentials: "same-origin",
    headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
    cache: "no-store",
    signal,
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Connection status request failed (${response.status}).`);

export type LichessConnectionStatus = {
  connected: boolean;
  configured: boolean;
};

export async function getLichessConnectionStatus(
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<LichessConnectionStatus> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/lichess/status", {
    method: "GET",
    credentials: "same-origin",
    headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
    cache: "no-store",
    signal,

export async function fetchOpeningExplorerStats(
  uciMoves: string[],
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<OpeningExplorerStats> {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();
  query.set("variant", "standard");
  query.set("moves", "8");
  if (uciMoves.length) query.set("play", uciMoves.join(","));

  const response = await fetch(
    `/api/lichess/opening-explorer?${query.toString()}`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      credentials: "same-origin",
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      signal,
    },
  );

========== STOCKFISH ENGINE ==========

--- MATCH: class StockfishEngine ---
}

export function whiteScoreShare(score: EngineScore): number {
  if (score.kind === "mate") return score.value > 0 ? 0.96 : 0.04;
  const bounded = Math.max(-1000, Math.min(1000, score.value));
  return Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.96, 1 / (1 + 10 ** (-bounded / 400))));
}

export class StockfishEngine {
  private readonly worker: Worker;
  private ready = false;
  private searching = false;
  private awaitingStop = false;
  private queued: UciRequest | null = null;
  private currentSide: "w" | "b" = "w";
  private latestScore: EngineScore | null = null;

  constructor(
    workerUrl: string,
    private readonly onStatus: (status: EngineStatus, message?: string) => void,
    private readonly onScore: (score: EngineScore | null) => void,
  ) {
    this.worker = new Worker(workerUrl);
    this.worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<string>) => this.handleLine(String(event.data));
    this.worker.onerror = (event) => {
      this.onStatus("error", event.message || "The Stockfish worker failed to load.");
      this.dispose();
------------------------------

--- MATCH: analyze ---
    this.worker.onerror = (event) => {
      this.onStatus("error", event.message || "The Stockfish worker failed to load.");
      this.dispose();
    };
    this.onStatus("loading");
    this.worker.postMessage("uci");
  }

  analyze(fen: string, depth: number): void {
    this.queued = { fen, depth: Math.max(1, Math.min(24, Math.floor(depth))) };
    this.latestScore = null;
    this.onScore(null);
    if (!this.ready) return;
    if (this.searching) {
      if (!this.awaitingStop) {
        this.awaitingStop = true;
        this.worker.postMessage("stop");
      }
      return;
    }
    this.startQueuedAnalysis();
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.worker.terminate();
    this.ready = false;
    this.searching = false;
------------------------------

  };
}

export function formatEvaluation(score: EngineScore): string {
  if (score.kind === "mate") return `#${score.value > 0 ? "+" : ""}${score.value}`;
  const pawns = score.value / 100;
  return `${pawns > 0 ? "+" : ""}${pawns.toFixed(2)}`;
}

export function whiteScoreShare(score: EngineScore): number {
  if (score.kind === "mate") return score.value > 0 ? 0.96 : 0.04;
  const bounded = Math.max(-1000, Math.min(1000, score.value));
  return Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.96, 1 / (1 + 10 ** (-bounded / 400))));
}

  private searching = false;

  private queued: UciRequest | null = null;
  private currentSide: "w" | "b" = "w";

export function formatEvaluation(score: EngineScore): string {
  if (score.kind === "mate") return `#${score.value > 0 ? "+" : ""}${score.value}`;
  const pawns = score.value / 100;
  return `${pawns > 0 ? "+" : ""}${pawns.toFixed(2)}`;
}

export function whiteScoreShare(score: EngineScore): number {
  if (score.kind === "mate") return score.value > 0 ? 0.96 : 0.04;
  const bounded = Math.max(-1000, Math.min(1000, score.value));
  return Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.96, 1 / (1 + 10 ** (-bounded / 400))));
}

    private readonly onScore: (score: EngineScore | null) => void,

--- MATCH: parseUciInfo ---
export type EngineStatus = "off" | "loading" | "ready" | "analyzing" | "error";
export type EngineScore = { kind: "cp" | "mate"; value: number; depth: number; bestMove?: string; pv?: string[] };

type UciRequest = { fen: string; depth: number };

export function parseUciInfo(line: string, sideToMove: "w" | "b"): Omit<EngineScore, "bestMove"> | null {
  if (!line.startsWith("info ")) return null;
  const depth = Number(line.match(/\bdepth (\d+)/)?.[1]);
  const score = line.match(/\bscore (cp|mate) (-?\d+)/);
  if (!score || !Number.isFinite(depth)) return null;
  const value = Number(score[2]);
  const whiteValue = sideToMove === "w" ? value : -value;
  const kind = score[1] as "cp" | "mate";
  const orientedValue = kind === "mate" && value === 0
    ? (sideToMove === "w" ? -1 : 1)
    : whiteValue;
  const pv = line.match(/\bpv (.+)$/)?.[1]?.trim().split(/\s+/);
  return {
    kind,
    value: orientedValue,
    depth,
    ...(pv?.length ? { pv } : {}),
  };
}

      this.ready = true;
      this.onStatus("ready");
      this.startQueuedAnalysis();
      return;
    }
    if (line.startsWith("info ")) {
      if (this.awaitingStop) return;
      const score = parseUciInfo(line, this.currentSide);
      if (score) {
        // Use the current principal variation's first move for a live arrow.
        // Stockfish's final "bestmove" will replace this provisional move.
        const provisionalMove = score.pv?.[0];
        this.latestScore = {
          ...score,
          ...(provisionalMove ? { bestMove: provisionalMove } : {}),
        };
        this.onScore(this.latestScore);
      }
      return;
    }
    const bestMove = line.match(/^bestmove (\S+)/)?.[1];
    if (!bestMove) return;
    this.searching = false;
    if (this.awaitingStop) {
      this.awaitingStop = false;
      this.startQueuedAnalysis();
